Faddeev-Kulish dressings render scattering amplitudes infrared-finite, but the resulting multi-particle states do not factorise into tensor products of dressed one-particle states. We show that this loss of Fock-space factorisation is not fundamental, but reflects an inappropriate choice of infrared variables. The real soft divergence is reproduced by the Goldstone modes of asymptotic symmetries, while the Coulomb phase is reproduced by new zero modes of the radiative fields that we introduce here. In these variables, infrared-finite dressed multi-particle states admit the usual Fock-space factorisation into single-particle dressed states.

</details>


### [101] [The O(4)-breaking bubble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16024)
*Guy Avraham,Kfir Blum,Omri Rosner,Isaac G. Smith*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在标量场势能$V(φ)=\frac{m^2}{2}φ^2-\fracλ{4}φ^4+\frac{g}{6}φ^6$中显式构造了一个非径向的假真空衰变解：两个符号相反的泡管缠绕在正交环上，具有${\rm O}(2)\times{\rm O}(2)$旋转对称性及环交换宇称，该解具有合法的实时演化柯西数据并支持奇数个不稳定变形模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越Coleman的O(4)对称反弹解，寻找并理解假真空衰变中O(4)破缺的非径向解及其物理意义，这类解在数学上已存在非构造性存在性证明但具体形式未知。

Method: 考虑有界下界的标量场势能模型，利用${\rm O}(2)\times{\rm O}(2)$对称性结合环交换宇称，显式构造非径向的假真空衰变解（双泡管结构）。

Result: 成功构造了一个非径向的假真空衰变解，该解在$t=0$切片上具有合法的柯西数据以支持实时演化，并拥有奇数个不稳定变形模式。

Conclusion: 假真空衰变中不仅存在Coleman的O(4)对称反弹解，还存在O(4)破缺的非径向解（如双泡管结构），这些解具有不同的对称性和动力学特征，丰富了假真空衰变的理论图景。

Abstract: False vacuum decay in field theory is thought to be dominated by Coleman's O(4)-symmetric bounce, the minimum action nontrivial solution of the imaginary time equations of motion. Beyond the bounce, non-constructive existence proofs of O(4)-breaking solutions are available in the mathematics literature, but the solutions themselves, and their physics, have remained unknown. Considering the simple, bounded-below, scalar field potential $V(φ)=\frac{m^2}{2}φ^2-\fracλ{4}φ^4+\frac{g}{6}φ^6$, we construct a nonradial solution explicitly: two bubble-tubes of opposite sign wrapping orthogonal rings, invariant under ${\rm O}(2)\times{\rm O}(2)$ rotations combined with a parity that exchanges the rings. The solution admits valid Cauchy data for real time evolution from a $t=0$ slice, and supports an odd number of unstable deformation modes.

</details>


### [102] [Effect of $ξRφ^2$ non-minimal coupling on gravitational light bending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16406)
*Ayan Kumar Naskar,Avijit Sen Majumder,Sourav Bhattacharya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了曲率-标量非最小耦合（√-g ξ R φ²）存在下，大质量物体对无质量场（标量场和光子）的弯曲效应，使用微扰量子引力计算至单圈阶。发现在树图阶（O(ξG)）无弯曲，领头阶出现在O(ξG²)，弯曲角行为∼b⁻⁷，且非最小顶点与标准最小耦合顶点有本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非最小耦合（曲率-标量耦合）在引力背景下对无质量场弯曲效应的量子修正，因为自相互作用标量场理论在存在引力时无法重整化，需要研究非最小耦合带来的新物理。

Method: 使用微扰量子引力计算，将大质量物体建模为非相对论性的大质量标量场，计算无质量标量场和光子通过引力子交换的2-2散射过程。假设ξ和弯曲角都很小，采用eikonal近似，计算至O(ξG²)阶。

Result: 树图阶（O(ξG)）无弯曲贡献，领头阶出现在O(ξG²)。弯曲角行为∼b⁻⁷（b为碰撞参数）。非最小耦合顶点包含引力子的显式动量而非标量动量，与标准最小耦合顶点定性不同。

Conclusion: 非最小耦合在量子引力弯曲效应中产生与最小耦合定性不同的结果，其领头阶出现在更高阶微扰，且弯曲角随碰撞参数衰减更快（b⁻⁷ vs 标准情况的更慢衰减），表明非最小耦合在长距离上影响更弱。

Abstract: We investigate the bending of massless fields by a massive object in the presence of a curvature-scalar $\sqrt{-g}ξR φ^2$ non-minimal coupling up to one loop, using the perturbative quantum gravity computations. It is well known that without such coupling a self interacting scalar field theory cannot be renormalised in the presence of gravity. The massive object is modelled by a massive scalar $φ$, and it is assumed to be non-relativistic, e.g., a star. We compute the 2-2 scattering of massless scalar and photons off this object via graviton exchanges. Assuming both $ξ$ and the bending angle to be small, we use the eikonal approximation to compute the angle up to ${\cal O}(ξG^2)$. At tree level $({\cal O}(ξG))$ we find no bending, and hence the ${\cal O}(ξG^2)$ result happens to be leading in this case. The non-minimal vertices are qualitatively different from that of the standard minimal ones, e.g. $ \sqrt{G} h_{μν} T^{μν}$, as the former contains explicit momenta of the gravitons instead of the scalar, complementing the second. The bending angle is found to behave like $\sim b^{-7}$, where $b$ is the impact parameter. We have emphasised the qualitative differences of our results from that of the well studied minimal case.

</details>


### [103] [Complexified Virasoro Flow and the Logarithmic Graviton at the Chiral Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16444)
*Yannick Mvondo-She*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了拓扑引力理论中，在手性点处对数引力子与复化Virasoro流之间的几何联系。通过分析对数引力子的系数函数，发现径向演化、时间演化、Jordan混合以及对数单值性可统一视为单一复化Virasoro流的不同表现形式，从而为AdS₃/LCFT₂对偶中的不可分解结构提供了几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑质量引力在手性点处存在对数模和秩二Jordan结构，该结构在AdS₃/LCFT₂对偶猜想中扮演关键角色。然而，目前对这种Jordan结构的几何起源缺乏统一理解，需要建立一种从几何角度解释对数模及其不可分解结构的框架。

Method: 从Grumiller-Johansson的对数引力子出发，推导其对数系数在AdS₃边界附近的渐近形式；引入复参数s=ρ+iτ，建立与Virasoro生成元L₀作用在秩二Jordan胞腔上的指数化关系；通过解析延拓s→s+2πi分析对数单值性。

Result: 发现对数引力子的对数系数在边界附近具有渐近形式y = -s + ln2 + O(e⁻²ρ)；同时Virasoro生成元L₀的指数化给出e^{sL₀} = e^{sh}(1+sN)，再现了对数扇区的特征混合行为；径向演化、时间演化、Jordan混合和对数单值性统一于单一复化Virasoro流。

Conclusion: 本文为对数CFT中典型的不可分解结构提供了几何解释，丰富了AdS₃/LCFT₂对偶框架的理论理解，揭示了拓扑质量引力中Jordan结构的深层几何本质。

Abstract: At the chiral point of topologically massive gravity, the massive graviton becomes degenerate with the left-moving graviton, leading to the appearance of a logarithmic mode and a corresponding rank-two Jordan structure. This logarithmic graviton plays a central role in the conjectured AdS$_3$/LCFT$_2$ correspondence, where it has been widely interpreted as the bulk counterpart of a logarithmic partner in the putative boundary logarithmic conformal field theory. In this work, we develop a geometric interpretation of this Jordan structure based on complexified Virasoro evolution. Starting from the logarithmic graviton of Grumiller and Johansson, we show that its logarithmic coefficient \[ y(τ,ρ) =
-iτ-\ln\coshρ\] admits, near the AdS$_3$ boundary, the asymptotic form \[ y(τ,ρ) =
-s+\ln2+\mathcal O(e^{-2ρ}), \qquad s=ρ+iτ. \] The same complex parameter naturally appears in the exponentiation of the Virasoro generator $L_0$ acting on a rank-two Jordan cell, \[ L_0=h\mathbf1+N, \qquad N^2=0, \] for which \[ e^{sL_0} =
e^{sh}(1+sN). \] We show that the resulting Jordan evolution reproduces the characteristic logarithmic mixing of the logarithmic sector, while analytic continuation $s\rightarrow s+2πi$ generates the corresponding logarithmic monodromy. From this perspective, radial evolution, temporal evolution, Jordan mixing, and logarithmic monodromy may be viewed as different manifestations of a single complexified Virasoro flow. The analysis suggests a geometric interpretation of the indecomposable structures characteristic of logarithmic conformal field theory and offers a new perspective on the logarithmic graviton within the conjectural AdS$_3$/LCFT$_2$ framework.

</details>


### [104] [Self-gravitating electromagnetic waves in the dark bubble model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16547)
*Ulf Danielsson,Daniel Panizo,Vincent Van Hemelryck*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文利用AdS5中的pp-wave几何构造了暗气泡场景中的引力波和电磁波嵌入模型，通过在三膜上粘合两个AdS5 pp-wave时空实现，并分析了局部光束及其引力反作用，发现引力修正与5d AdS尺度处4d引力减弱一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于pp-wave时空通常提供运动方程的精确解这一事实，在暗气泡场景中嵌入引力和电磁波，以研究其动力学行为。

Method: 利用AdS5中的pp-wave几何，通过在三膜上粘合两个AdS5 pp-wave时空来实现设置，并施加合适的混合边界条件进行分析。

Result: 施加合适的混合边界条件后，发现了与5d AdS尺度处4d引力减弱相一致的引力修正。

Conclusion: 该构造为暗气泡场景中引力和电磁波的动力学提供了精确解框架，并揭示了引力在AdS尺度下的修正效应。

Abstract: We construct embeddings of gravitational and electromagnetic waves in the dark bubble scenario using pp-wave geometries in AdS5, motivated by the fact that pp-wave spacetimes often provide exact solutions to the equations of motion. The setup is realised by gluing two AdS5 pp-wave spacetimes across a three-brane. As an application, we analyse localised beams of light and their gravitational backreaction. Imposing suitable mixed boundary conditions in AdS5, we find gravitational corrections consistent with a weakening of 4d gravity at the 5d AdS scale.

</details>


### [105] [Flux Quantization on 10D Type IIA Superspace via Cyclification from 11D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16857)
*Grigorios Giotopoulos,Hisham Sati*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明：通过循环化（cyclification），11D超引力在无挠超空间上仅通过C场超通量密度的对偶对称Bianchi恒等式即可确定，其维度约化给出10D IIA超引力等价于$\mathfrak{l}\mathrm{Cyc}(S^4)$-Bianchi恒等式约束NS/RR超通量密度。这一机制推广为：对偶对称超空间Bianchi恒等式由分类空间$\mathcal{A}$的Whitehead括号$L_\infty$-代数$\mathfrak{l}\mathcal{A}$刻画时，维度约化由循环环路空间$\mathrm{Cyc}(\mathcal{A})$刻画，且与通量量子化的非交换上同调全局完备化相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立从11D超引力到10D IIA超引力的严格维度约化机制，揭示对偶对称Bianchi恒等式与$L_\infty$-代数结构之间的深层联系，并为超引力理论的通量量子化全局完备化提供统一的数学框架。

Method: 利用循环化（cyclification）操作将11D无挠超空间上的C场超通量密度Bianchi恒等式约化到10D；通过分类空间$\mathcal{A}$的Whitehead括号$L_\infty$-代数$\mathfrak{l}\mathcal{A}$与循环环路空间$\mathrm{Cyc}(\mathcal{A})$的$L_\infty$-代数$\mathfrak{l}\mathrm{Cyc}(\mathcal{A})$之间的对应关系建立一般性维度约化机制。

Result: 11D超引力等价于$\mathfrak{l}S^4$-Bianchi恒等式；10D IIA超引力等价于$\mathfrak{l}\mathrm{Cyc}(S^4)$-Bianchi恒等式；任何$\mathbb{R}$-有理等价于$\mathrm{Cyc}(S^4)$的空间分类可接受的通量量子化律，提供可氧化到11D的10D IIA超引力全局完备化。

Conclusion: 对偶对称超空间Bianchi恒等式的$L_\infty$-代数刻画为超引力理论的维度约化与通量量子化全局完备化提供了统一框架，11D与10D IIA超引力之间通过循环化建立了严格的代数等价关系。

Abstract: We produce the dimensional reduction to 10D IIA supergravity (SuGra), via cyclification, of the remarkable result that full 11D SuGra is put on shell just by imposing the duality-symmetric Bianchi identities on C-field super-flux densities over supertorsion-free superspace.
  Generally, we highlight that when duality-symmetric superspace Bianchi identities are characterized by Whitehead bracket $L_\infty$-algebras $\mathfrak{l}\mathcal{A}$ of a classifying space $\mathcal{A}$, their dimensional reduction is characterized by the cyclic loop space $\mathrm{Cyc}(\mathcal{A})$. We promote this to a general mechanism of dimensional reduction on super-spacetime, compatible with the global (infrared) completion of supergravity theories by flux quantization in non-abelian cohomology with coefficients in $\mathcal{A}$ and $\mathrm{Cyc}(\mathcal{A})$, respectively.
  In the case of 11D SuGra, the characteristic $L_\infty$-algebra is $\mathfrak{l}S^4$ and hence we obtain that full on-shell 10D IIA SuGra is equivalent to $\mathfrak{l}\mathrm{Cyc}(S^4)$-Bianchi imposed identities on NS/RR super-flux densities over supertorsion-free 10D super-spacetime. This implies that any space which is $\mathbb{R}$-rationally equivalent to $\mathrm{Cyc}(S^4)$ classifies an admissible flux quantization law, which provides a global completion of 10D IIA SuGra that admits oxidation to 11D.

</details>


### [106] [The supergravity dual of a finite duality cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16630)
*Pietro Moroni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文总结了arXiv:2506.18988的主要结果，研究在D3-探针与分数量子膜构成的𝒩=1规范理论中，添加Orientifold平面如何将Klebanov-Strassler模型的无限级联RG流修改为有限步数的级联，并给出了相应的超引力对偶解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在𝒩=1规范理论中，添加Orientifold平面对级联RG流结构的影响，特别是如何将无限级联修改为有限步数级联。

Method: 基于arXiv:2506.18988的理论框架，构建超引力对偶解来模拟和验证有限步数级联RG流的全部特征。

Result: Orientifold平面的加入改变了原有级联结构，使得RG流从具有有限自由度的UV不动点出发，经历有限步数后终止于禁闭相。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解Orientifold对规范理论级联RG流的影响提供了超引力对偶描述，丰富了𝒩=1规范理论的非微扰动力学研究。

Abstract: This proceedings contribution summarizes the main results of the original paper arXiv:2506.18988. Cascading RG flows are characteristic of $\mathcal{N}=1$ gauge theories realized by D3-branes probing singularities in the presence of fractional branes. A celebrated example is the Klebanov-Strassler model, which exhibits an infinite cascade that ends with confinement. In this work, we explore a related setup where the addition of an orientifold plane modifies the cascade structure: the RG flow now consists of a finite number of steps, originating from a UV fixed point with a finite number of degrees of freedom. We provide a supergravity solution dual to this flow, that reproduces all its salient features.

</details>


### [107] [Holographic study of heavy quark potential, free energy, and running coupling in backgrounds with broken translational symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16640)
*Wenxing Cheng,Zi-qiang Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 通过五维渐近AdS时空中的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-轴子模型，研究了具有平移对称性破缺的强耦合夸克胶子等离子体中重夸克观测量（静态势、热自由能、跑动耦合），发现化学势和耗散参数通过不同机制削弱色相互作用并促进夸克偶素解离，其中化学势主导色屏蔽，而耗散参数主要修正熵和热自由能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清在具有有限重子密度和动量弛豫的耗散性等离子体中，色相互作用和热力学性质的介质修正机制，为重夸克探针提供一致的物理图像。

Method: 采用五维渐近AdS时空中的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-轴子模型，引入化学势μ（有限重子密度）和平移对称性破缺参数β（动量弛豫），通过有限温度和零温度下的数值模拟研究重夸克观测量。

Result: μ和β均削弱色相互作用并促进夸克偶素解离，但机制不同：μ主导色屏蔽并修正重夸克势和跑动耦合，β主要影响等离子体熵并修正热自由能。有限重子密度对Q\bar{Q}束缚的抑制远强于动量耗散。零温度下重整化自由能退化为近似库仑形式的介质修正静态势。

Conclusion: 该工作澄清了色相互作用和热力学性质的介质修正机制，为重夸克在稠密耗散等离子体中的探针行为提供了一致的物理图像，并提取了色屏蔽长度和解离尺度，对重离子碰撞中的夸克偶素物理具有现象学意义。

Abstract: We study heavy-quark observables including static interquark potential, thermal free energy and running coupling via a five-dimensional asymptotically AdS spacetime with translational symmetry breaking (TSB). The Einstein-Maxwell-axion geometry involves two scales: chemical potential $μ$ for finite baryon density, and TSB parameter $β$ for momentum relaxation. Numerical simulations at finite and zero temperature reveal that both $μ$ and $β$ weaken color interactions and facilitate quarkonium dissociation in strongly coupled quark-gluon plasmas through different mechanisms. The chemical potential dominates color screening and modifies the heavy-quark potential and running coupling, while $β$ mainly affects plasma entropy and corrects thermal free energy. At zero temperature, thermal contributions vanish, and the renormalized free energy becomes a medium-modified static potential with an approximate Coulombic form. Finite baryon density suppresses $Q\bar{Q}$ binding much more strongly than momentum dissipation at all temperatures. We extract the color screening length and dissociation scale, and discuss phenomenological implications for quarkonium in heavy-ion collisions. This work clarifies medium correction mechanisms for color interactions and thermodynamics, and presents a consistent picture for heavy-quark probes in dense dissipative plasmas.

</details>


### [108] [O(N) BCFT: new data from conformal partial wave expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14733)
*Jozef Csipes,Petr Vaško*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用大N展开分析O(N) BCFT，研究边界条件对应普通相变，通过共形分波展开计算了bulk 2-点函数⟨φφ⟩、⟨φ²φ²⟩和⟨σσ⟩，证明了边界谱函数的猜想，重现了已知结果，并首次给出⟨σσ⟩的bulk展开，提取了未知OPE系数C_{σσσ³}的领先大N表达式，同时得到了与已有结果一致的混合系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在O(N) BCFT的大N展开框架下，系统地计算和分析边界谱函数，验证先前工作中的猜想，并提取未知的OPE系数，以进一步理解边界CFT的数据结构。

Method: 采用大N展开方法，在bulk维度2<d+1<4下分析O(N) BCFT。将bulk 2-点函数⟨φφ⟩、⟨φ²φ²⟩和⟨σσ⟩展开为bulk/边界共形分波，通过谱函数编码BCFT数据并确定共形块展开。利用该方法验证了Dujava等人的猜想，重现了Giombi等人的结果，并首次计算了⟨σσ⟩的bulk展开以提取未知OPE系数。

Result: 成功证明了关于边界谱函数的猜想，重现了Giombi等人的⟨φφ⟩的bulk展开；首次得到⟨σσ⟩的bulk展开，提取了OPE系数C_{σσσ³}的领先大N表达式；同时得到的混合系数与Derkachov等人的结果一致，支持了主要结论的可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文方法可推广到特殊或超常相变边界条件，虽能产生无限多个约束条件，但由于算子混合随简并度增加而变得复杂，需要额外输入才能完全解耦。

Abstract: The $\mathsf{O}(N)$ $\mathsf{BCFT}$ is analyzed in the large $N$ expansion in a generic bulk dimension $2<d+1<4$. Focus is on boundary conditions corresponding to the ordinary transition, however techniques used can be generalized to special or extraordinary transitions. We study the system of bulk $2$-pt functions $\langle φφ\rangle$, $\langle φ^2 φ^2\rangle$ and $\langle σσ\rangle$, where $σ$ is the Hubbard--Stratonovich field. They are expanded in bulk/boundary conformal partial waves. The coefficient functions of this expansion -- the spectral functions -- encode $\mathsf{BCFT}$ data and determine bulk/boundary conformal block expansions. We prove conjectures about boundary spectral functions in Dujava et al. [arXiv:2503.16345] and reproduce the bulk expansion of $\langle φφ\rangle$ in Giombi et al. [arXiv:2007.04955]. The bulk expansion of $\langleσσ\rangle$ is new and allows to extract the leading large $N$ expression for an unknown OPE coefficient $C_{σσσ^3}$ (the computation outputs as a byproduct also a mixing coefficient, which matches an existing result by Derkachov et al. [arXiv:hep-th/9705020], providing further support for the main result). Besides these derived data infinitely many constraints are produced, though due to operator mixing growing in complexity as degeneracy increases, they cannot be disentangled without further input.

</details>


### [109] [On the Representation Theory of Non-Admissible $W$-Algebras: Part I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16714)
*Dan Xie*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于圆紧致化4d N=2理论的镜像对称性，提出利用广义仿射Springer纤维的几何框架研究非容许W-代数W^k(g,f)在特定水平下的表示理论，并将C^*不动点与简单模和非半单结构（对数模）对应起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受圆紧致化4d N=2理论的镜像对称性启发，旨在为非容许W-代数的表示论提供几何框架，弥补此前只适用于容许水平的局限性。

Method: 利用广义仿射Springer纤维Sp_ν(̃g,f)的几何，通过C^*作用的不动点集（由双陪集ᵝw标记）构造简单模，其最高权由ᵝw↦ᵝw(kΛ_0+ᵝρ)-ᵝρ确定，不动点簇的维数编码非半单结构（对数模）。

Result: 在D_4、E_6、E_8以及扭型理论^3D_4和^2A_3等多个例子中验证了该对应关系，几何计数复现了容许和非容许W-代数的已知结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何框架能够统一处理容许与非容许水平的W-代数表示论，并揭示了非半单结构（对数模）的几何起源。

Abstract: Motivated by the mirror symmetry for circle compactified 4d $N=2$ theories, we propose a geometric framework for studying the representation theory of non-admissible $W$-algebras $W^{k}(\mathfrak g,f)$ at levels $k=-h^\vee+\frac{1}{n}\frac{m}{u}$, using the geometry of generalized affine Springer fibers $Sp_ν(\tilde{g},f)$ with slope $ν=u/m$. The central proposal is that each non-empty $C^*$-fixed locus, labeled by a double coset $\tilde w\in W_ν\backslash\tilde{W}/W_f$, gives rise to simple modules whose highest weight is determined by the map $\tilde w\mapsto\tilde w(kΛ_0+\tildeρ)-\tildeρ$, while the dimension of the fixed variety encodes additional non-semisimple structure (logarithmic modules). We verify this correspondence in numerous examples, including $D_4$, $E_6$, $E_8$, and twisted theories of type $^3D_4$ and $^2A_3$, where our geometric counting reproduces known results for both admissible and non-admissible $W$-algebras.

</details>


### [110] [Effective Lifshitz-Born-Infeld black holes from general covariance breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16716)
*D. C. Moreira,A. S. Lemos,F. A. Brito*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带有Born-Infeld电动力学的有效Lifshitz黑洞解，并通过破缺微分同胚不变性捕获该解，研究了其因果结构、临界行为及热力学局部稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索带有Born-Infeld电动力学的Lifshitz黑洞解及其性质，拓展对非相对论性全息对偶中引力系统的理解。

Method: 通过破缺微分同胚不变性机制构建有效的Lifshitz黑洞解，结合Born-Infeld电动力学进行分析。

Result: 成功获得了有效的Lifshitz黑洞解，揭示了其因果结构，并分析了临界行为和热力学局部稳定性。

Conclusion: 该解为研究Lifshitz全息对偶中的非相对论性引力系统提供了新的理论工具，并展现了丰富的热力学行为。

Abstract: In this work we present an effective Lifshitz black hole solution with Born-Infeld electrodynamics and explore some of its properties. We discuss the mechanism for capturing the solution, achieved through diffeomorphism invariance breaking, study the emergent causal structure, and analyze aspects of critical behavior and local stability in the associated thermodynamics.

</details>


### [111] [Effective Goldstone dynamics on cosmological space-times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16745)
*Marc Schneider*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了闵可夫斯基时空上有质量自旋-2粒子的LSZ约化形式，并将其扩展到宇宙学时空。该约化形式为证明FLRW时空中Goldstone玻色子等价定理提供了通用方法。针对德西特和辐射填充宇宙，研究了Goldstone动力学，并分析了有效动力学算子的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有质量自旋-2粒子的LSZ约化形式从闵可夫斯基时空推广到宇宙学时空（如FLRW、德西特和辐射填充宇宙），并利用该形式证明Goldstone玻色子等价定理在膨胀宇宙中的成立性。

Method: 1. 在闵可夫斯基时空上推导有质量自旋-2粒子的LSZ约化形式；2. 将该约化形式推广到宇宙学（FLRW）时空；3. 利用约化形式证明Goldstone玻色子等价定理；4. 对德西特和辐射填充宇宙进行Goldstone动力学分析及有效算子的适用范围评估。

Result: 成功将LSZ约化形式推广到宇宙学时空，并证明了Goldstone玻色子等价定理在FLRW时空中的有效性；在德西特和辐射填充宇宙中，完成了Goldstone动力学的分析及有效动力学算子适用范围的研究。

Conclusion: 本文建立的LSZ约化形式为研究宇宙学背景下的有质量自旋-2粒子散射过程提供了理论工具，并验证了Goldstone玻色子等价定理在膨胀宇宙中的成立性，对理解早期宇宙中引力子物理有重要意义。

Abstract: We derive the Lehmann-Symanzik-Zimmermann reduction formalism for a massive spin-2 particle on Minkowski spacetime and extend the formalism to cosmological spacetimes. The reduction formalism allows for a versatile proof that the Goldstone boson equivalence theorem holds in Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker space-times. For the de Sitter and the radiation filled universe, we investigate the Goldstone dynamics and perform an analysis of the range of validity provided by the effective kinetic operator.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum gravity and spectral running cutoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16911)
*Carlo Branchina,Vincenzo Branchina,Filippo Contino,Riccardo Gandolfo,Arcangelo Pernace*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过引入协变拉普拉斯算符本征值的“谱截断”来实现威尔逊范式，并将其应用于量子引力。通过“硬”和“平滑”两种谱截断实现，推导了牛顿常数和宇宙学常数的重整化群方程，发现了渐近安全场景中的非高斯紫外吸引固定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前已证明在规范理论中通过引入“谱截断”（即对协变拉普拉斯算符本征值进行截断）可以自然地实现威尔逊范式，为重整化群构建提供途径。本文旨在将该思想推广至量子引力领域。

Method: 采用两种谱截断实现方式：“硬截断”和“平滑截断”。基于协变拉普拉斯算符的谱，推导牛顿常数和宇宙学常数的重整化群方程。

Result: 得到了渐近安全场景的重整化群图案，确认存在一个非高斯型紫外吸引固定点。

Conclusion: 谱截断方法可有效应用于量子引力的重整化群分析，并为渐近安全场景提供支持证据。

Abstract: We have recently shown that a natural way to implement the Wilsonian paradigm in gauge theories is through the introduction of a ``spectral cutoff", a cut on the eigenvalues of the covariant Laplacian, pointing out that this provides the route toward the renormalization group (RG) construction. Here we apply this idea to quantum gravity, resorting to two realizations of the spectral running cutoff: ``hard" and ``smooth". We derive the RG equations for the Newton and cosmological constant and find the RG pattern of the asymptotic safety scenario, with a non-Gaussian UV-attractive fixed point.

</details>


### [113] [The black hole at the end of the cone: localizing the anomaly polynomial on toric geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16986)
*Davide Cassani,Enrico Turetta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效方法，通过将异常多项式六形式在环面图上进行等变积分，将超对称黑鞍解的在场作用量表示为各锥顶贡献之和，从而简洁推导了黑洞、黑环、黑透镜等解的在场作用量和Wald熵，适用于带或不带高导修正的五维超引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五维超引力中，超对称黑鞍解的在场作用量计算通常繁琐，现有方法缺乏统一性和高效性。本文旨在提出一种基于环面图和等变积分的通用方法，简化计算并推广到新构型。

Method: 将异常多项式六形式在环面图上进行等变积分，通过将环面图分解为单纯锥，使在场作用量表示为各锥顶贡献的求和；该方法同时适用于规范化和非规范化情况，以及高导修正。

Result: 获得了已有表达式的简洁推导，并给出了黑鞍解的在场作用量和相关极值解的Wald熵的新预测；示例涵盖黑洞、黑环、黑透镜以及拓扑孤子背景中的黑洞，包括渐近AdS$_5$和渐近平坦情形。

Conclusion: 该方法为五维超引力中具有环面对称性的超对称黑鞍解提供了统一的在场作用量计算公式，兼具简洁性和推广能力，可用于推导新解的热力学量。

Abstract: We consider five-dimensional supergravity coupled to vector multiplets, gauged or ungauged, and propose an efficient method to evaluate the on-shell action of supersymmetric black saddle solutions with toric ${\rm U}(1)^3$ symmetry and general topology, explicitly known or just assumed to exist, including higher-derivative corrections. This is equivalent to equivariant integration of the anomaly polynomial six-form over simplicial cones obtained by decomposing the toric diagram of the solution. The on-shell action is then expressed as a sum over contributions localized at the tip of each cone. We obtain a simple derivation of recently calculated expressions as well as new predictions, both for the on-shell action of the black saddles and the Wald entropy of the related extremal solutions. These may be asymptotically AdS$_5$ or asymptotically flat. As examples, we discuss black holes, black rings and black lenses, including a black hole in the background of a topological soliton.

</details>


### [114] [Solutions in Liouville theory on dS and AdS backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17025)
*Damir Sadekov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在D维德西特和反德西特背景（非动力学几何）下找到了Liouville型标量场理论的精确解，利用嵌入空间形式主义和辅助零向量导出了可积的一阶Bäcklund类方程，得到了两类解析解，它们在德西特和反德西特时空中的物理性质不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空背景下求解非线性标量场理论（Liouville型）的精确解，特别是对于具有最大对称性的德西特和反德西特时空，以理解这类理论在不同背景下的物理行为。

Method: 采用嵌入空间形式主义，引入辅助零向量，将原Liouville方程降阶为一组一阶Bäcklund类方程，通过保证该一阶方程组的可积性来构造原方程的精确解析解。

Result: 得到了两类解析解族，并发现这些解在dS和AdS时空中展现出截然不同的物理性质，表明背景曲率的符号对解的结构有决定性影响。

Conclusion: 本文提出的嵌入空间方法为在弯曲时空背景下求解Liouville型标量场理论提供了系统化的解析途径，揭示了解在dS和AdS背景下的本质差异。

Abstract: We find exact solutions of a Liouville-type scalar field theory on $D$-dimensional de Sitter and anti-de Sitter backgrounds, treating the geometry as nondynamical. Using the embedding-space formalism with an auxiliary null vector, we derive first-order (Bäcklund-like) equations whose integrability yields the solution of the Liouville equation for the scalar field. This method produces two classes of analytic solutions, whose physical properties are different for de Sitter and anti-de Sitter spacetimes.

</details>

### [115] [A Reverse Hard-Core Model in $\mathbb{A}_2$: an Application of the Pirogov-Sinai Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14903)
*A. Mazel,I. Stuhl,Y. Suhov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Pirogov-Sinai理论分析单位三角晶格上的一类统计力学粒子模型，证明了其周期基态由特定边长平方的三角子格构成，并在足够小的活动参数下描述了相变结构和纯相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有排斥性约束（禁止空心圆盘）的粒子模型在三角晶格上的基态构型和相变行为，揭示粒子活性与最大清空半径之间的竞争机制。

Method: 采用Pirogov-Sinai理论分析模型，引入两个参数（粒子活性u和Lösch数t），通过周期性基态的显式构造和极小化哈密顿量，结合Gibbs（DLR）测度理论分析相结构。

Result: 证明了周期基态是占据位形成具有显式确定的边长平方d*(t)的三角子格的构型；对于足够小的u值，某些（但不一定是全部）周期基态生成极值Gibbs测度。

Conclusion: 描述了模型的相结构，刻画了与稳定基态相关的纯相，给出了在竞争性约束下三角晶格上粒子系统相变的完整图景。

Abstract: In this paper we use the Pirogov--Sinai theory to analyze a class of particle models of Statistical Mechanics on the unit triangular lattice $\mathbb{A}_2$. The models are specified by two parameters: the particle activity $u\in(0,1)$ and a positive Löschian number $t$ interpreted as a maximal squared clearing radius. Admissible configurations are those in which every empty lattice site has an occupied site within squared distance at most $t$, thereby forbidding empty disks of radius exceeding or equal $t$. The Hamiltonian favors configurations with as few occupied sites as possible, while the admissibility condition enforces a positive density of particles.
  We prove that the periodic ground states of the model, i.e., the configurations achieving an optimal balance of two tendencies, are the ones whose occupied sites form triangular sublattices of explicitly determined squared side-length $d_*(t)$. Furthermore, for sufficiently small values of the activity parameter $u$, some (but not necessarily all) periodic ground states generate extreme Gibbs (DLR) measures. We describe the resulting phase structure and characterize the pure phases associated with stable ground states.

</details>


### [116] [Structure-Informed Neural Operators for Long-Time Prediction of Parametric Hamiltonian PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14913)
*Victory C. Obieke,Christopher Chukwuemeka,Emmanuel E. Oguadimma*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出能量投影傅里叶神经算子(EP-FNO)，通过结合残差FNO时间步进更新与不变量投影，实现对参数化哈密顿偏微分方程的长时间稳定预测，并在ZK、KP、sine-Gordon方程上验证了其优于标准FNO的长时间稳定性和波形传播精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准傅里叶神经算子(FNO)在自回归 rollout 过程中无法保持哈密顿PDE的质量、动量和能量等守恒量，导致漂移、相位误差和定性精度损失，需要一种能保持不变量的学习架构。

Method: 提出EP-FNO架构，包含残差FNO时间步进更新模块和不变量的投影修正机制，并提供算子逼近效率的理论分析和稳定性估计。

Result: 在Zakharov-Kuznetsov、Kadomtsev-Petviashvili和sine-Gordon方程上的数值实验表明，投影模型相比标准FNO基线显著提升了长时间稳定性和孤子与相干波结构的传播精度。

Conclusion: 不变量投影有效提升了学习代理模型在长时间哈密顿PDE模拟中的可靠性。

Abstract: Hamiltonian partial differential equations (PDEs) often exhibit long-time dynamics governed by conserved quantities such as mass, momentum, and Hamiltonian energy. Standard Fourier neural operators (FNOs) provide efficient data-driven approximations of solution operators, but may not preserve these invariants during autoregressive rollout, and can develop drift in conserved quantities, phase error, and loss of qualitative accuracy. We propose an energy-projection Fourier neural operator (EP-FNO), a structure-informed operator learning architecture that combines a residual FNO time-stepping update with an invariant projection for long-time prediction of parametric Hamiltonian PDEs. We also provide a theoretical analysis showing that EP-FNO can approximate operators associated with PDEs efficiently, we also suggest a stability estimate.
  We evaluate the approach on the Zakharov--Kuznetsov, Kadomtsev--Petviashvili, and sine--Gordon equations. Numerical experiments show that the projected model improves long-time stability, and gives more accurate propagation of soliton and coherent wave structures compared with a standard FNO baseline. Our results demonstrate that invariant projection improves the reliability of learned surrogates for long-time Hamiltonian PDE simulation.

</details>


### [117] [Dynamic Doppler Effects on Dirac Spinor Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14952)
*Bryce M. Barclay,Alex Mahalov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了非惯性观测者运动对狄拉克旋量场的动态多普勒效应，揭示了固有加速度、固有急动度、路径曲率、挠率和超挠率如何修正自由粒子的惯性量子描述，并发现旋量结构产生的振幅超指数增长（$\exp(j_0τ^2/4)$）及自旋诱导相位因子等标量场不具备的独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解非惯性观测者运动对狄拉克旋量场的动态多普勒效应，填补标量克莱因-戈登场研究中未涵盖的自旋诱导效应的理论空白。

Method: 通过分析非惯性观测者世界线的Frenet-Serret曲率不变量，推导狄拉克旋量场振幅和相位的非线性行为，量化固有加速度、急动度、路径曲率、挠率和超挠率的贡献，并与标量场进行对比。

Result: 发现恒定固有急动度下振幅呈超指数增长（$\exp(j_0τ^2/4)$）；Frenet-Serret曲率不变量同时产生指数振幅因子和自旋诱导相位因子；旋量结构产生标量场无法预测的可观测动态多普勒特征。

Conclusion: 非惯性观测者的旋量场动态多普勒效应包含独特的自旋诱导特征，超出标量场理论的预测范围，具有可观测的物理意义。

Abstract: We derive the dynamic Doppler effects of noninertial observer motion on Dirac spinor fields, characterizing how proper acceleration, proper jerk, observer path curvature, torsion, and hyper-torsion modify the inertial quantum description of free particles. We quantify the nonlinear behavior of the amplitude and phase of the Dirac spinor wave field and isolate the spin-induced signatures that have no counterpart in the scalar Klein-Gordon field. For worldlines with constant proper jerk the amplitude scales super-exponentially as $\exp(j_0τ^2/4)$. The complex Frenet-Serret curvature invariants generate both an exponential amplitude factor and a spin-induced phase factor. Spinor structure therefore produces an observable dynamic Doppler signature distinct from any scalar prediction.

</details>


### [118] [On the Non-Commutative Geometry of the Electroweak Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15039)
*Gustavo Amilcar Saldaña Moncada*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用非交换几何，将电弱理论描述为在闵可夫斯基度量下，建立在$C^\infty_\C(\R^4)$上的平凡量子主$SU(2)$-丛，从而将经典微分几何中的$(SU(2)\times U(1))$-主丛结构简化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典微分几何中，电弱理论背后的数学结构是带闵可夫斯基度量的$\R^4$上的平凡主$(SU(2)\times U(1))$-丛。本文旨在展示，利用非交换几何，可以用更简洁的量子主丛结构来描述电弱理论。

Method: 采用非交换几何的方法，将经典的主丛结构重构为建立在$C^\infty_\C(\R^4)$上的平凡量子主$SU(2)$-丛，并保留闵可夫斯基度量。

Result: 成功将电弱理论的数学基础从经典微分几何中的$(SU(2)\times U(1))$-主丛转化为非交换几何框架下的$SU(2)$-量子主丛。

Conclusion: 非交换几何为电弱理论提供了新的数学描述，将原来的$(SU(2)\times U(1))$规范群结构简化为$SU(2)$量子主丛，展示了非交换几何在理论物理中的应用潜力。

Abstract: In differential geometry, the underlying mathematical structure behind the electroweak theory is a trivial principal $(SU(2)\times U(1))$--bundle over $\R^4$ with the Minkowski metric. The aim of this paper is to show that, using non--commutative geometry, the electroweak theory can be described using a trivial quantum principal $SU(2)$--bundle over $C^\infty_\C(\R^4)$ with the Minkowski metric.

</details>


### [119] [The Standard Model Gauge Group from the Exceptional Jordan Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15235)
*John C. Baez,Paul Schwahn*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用例外Jordan代数 $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ 及其自同构群 $\text{F}_4$ 构造了标准模型规范群。具体而言，对于满足 $X \cong \mathfrak{h}_2(\mathbb{C})$ 和 $B \cong \mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{C})$ 的子代数对 $X \subset B \subset \mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$，$X$ 的稳定化子与 $B$ 的稳定化子单位连通分支的交集同构于标准模型规范群。这可以理解为：八元数qutrit的对称性中，限制为普通qutrit上的幺正算子、再进一步限制为qubit上的幺正算子的部分，恰好构成标准模型规范群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找标准模型规范群在数学上的深层根源，特别是与例外代数结构和量子信息中的量子比特、量子三比特的Jordan代数表示之间的联系。

Method: 利用例外Jordan代数 $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$（八元数qutrit的观察代数）及其自同构群 $\text{F}_4$ 的群作用，分析子代数对 $X \cong \mathfrak{h}_2(\mathbb{C})$（qubit）包含于 $B \cong \mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{C})$（qutrit）包含于 $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ 的稳定化子结构，通过取交集得到标准模型规范群。

Result: 标准模型规范群可以表示为 $\text{F}_4$ 中同时稳定一个qubit子代数和一个qutrit子代数的元素构成的子群。

Conclusion: 标准模型规范群可以从八元数qutrit的对称性中自然导出，为理解标准模型的数学基础提供了新的视角，并揭示了量子信息理论中的qubit/qutrit结构与粒子物理规范对称性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We construct the Standard Model gauge group using the exceptional Jordan algebra $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ and its automorphism group $\text{F}_4$. The group $\text{F}_4$ acts on pairs of Jordan subalgebras $X \subset B \subset \mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ with $X \cong \mathfrak{h}_2(\mathbb{C})$ and $B \cong \mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{C})$, and for any such pair the stabilizer of $X$ intersected with the identity component of the stabilizer of $B$ is isomorphic to the Standard Model gauge group. Since $\mathfrak{h}_2(\mathbb{C})$ is the Jordan algebra of observables of a qubit and $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{C})$ is the Jordan algebra of observables of a qutrit, we could say $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ is the Jordan algebra of observables of an 'octonionic qutrit'. In this language our result says roughly that the Standard Model gauge group is the group of symmetries of an octonionic qutrit that restrict to act as unitary operators on an ordinary qutrit and, within that, a qubit.

</details>


### [120] [Generalized symmetries, invariant solutions and conservation laws in the Jaynes-Cummings model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15538)
*Luis M. Piñuelas,Pablo C. López Vázquez,Alexander Yakhno*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文运用李对称分析与守恒律理论研究Jaynes-Cummings模型，将其转化为偏微分方程组，确定了点对称性和广义对称性，构建了满足量子力学物理条件的不变解。除恢复传统缀饰态解外，还得到了用Heun多项式表达的径向依赖解。通过生成函数方法导出局部守恒律，包括总激发数守恒、原子布居、相干性、约化态纯度及场特征函数矩的守恒流，并推导了原子纯度与相干性的平衡方程，其演化受原子-场耦合控制，与原子-场关联和纠缠动力学相关。对称性结构还生成了广义对称性和无限守恒律层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将李对称分析和守恒律理论应用于Jaynes-Cummings模型，从微分方程对称性角度揭示该量子光学基本模型的深层数学结构，并探索对称性与原子-场纠缠动力学之间的内在联系。

Method: 将冯·诺依曼方程投影到原子自由度上，并用特征函数表示场模，转化为偏微分方程组；应用李对称分析确定点对称性和广义对称性；利用生成函数方法推导局部守恒律。

Result: 恢复了传统缀饰态解，并获得了一类用Heun多项式表达的具有径向依赖的新型解；除总激发数守恒外，还得到了原子布居、相干性、约化态纯度及场特征函数矩的守恒流；推导出原子纯度与相干性的平衡方程，其演化受原子-场耦合控制并与纠缠动力学相关；发现了广义对称性和无限守恒律层级。

Conclusion: 李对称分析为JCM提供了新的解析解（Heun多项式解），守恒律分析揭示了原子纯度-相干性平衡方程与纠缠动力学的关联，对称性结构导出了无限守恒律层级，深化了对该模型数学结构和物理内涵的理解。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the Jaynes--Cummings model (JCM) using Lie symmetry analysis and conservation-law theory. The dynamics is formulated as a system of partial differential equations by projecting the von Neumann equation onto the atomic degrees of freedom and representing the field mode through its characteristic function. We determine the admitted point and generalized symmetries and construct invariant solutions satisfying the physical conditions imposed by quantum mechanics. The conventional dressed-state dynamics is recovered while a second class of solutions with radial dependence expressed through Heun polynomials is obtained for coupled atom--field configurations.
  We also apply the generating functions methodology to derive local conservation laws of the JCM differential system. Besides recovering the conservation of the total number of excitations, we obtain additional conserved currents involving atomic populations, coherence, reduced-state purity, and moments of the field characteristic function. In particular, we derive a balance equation for a combination of atomic purity and coherence whose evolution is controlled by the atom--field coupling and is linked to atom--field correlation and entanglement dynamics. The symmetry structure further generates generalized symmetries and an infinite hierarchy of conservation laws.

</details>


### [121] [A High-Order Nyström Method for Coupled Boundary Integral Equations in Oblique-Incidence Scattering by Impedance Cylinders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15616)
*Haochen Liu,Qinghao Yu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对斜入射条件下无限长阻抗圆柱的电磁散射问题，构建了一种基于Kress型对数核分解、周期乘积求积、傅里叶微分和块对角预条件子的高阶Nyström数值方法，并通过数值实验验证了该方法的稳定性和高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决斜入射条件下无限长阻抗圆柱电磁散射问题中，耦合的二维Helmholtz方程与Leontovich阻抗条件带来的数值求解困难，特别是对数核与主值切向导数项的处理。

Method: 基于已有耦合积分方程公式，采用Kress型对数核分解、周期乘积求积、傅里叶微分处理切向导数贡献，并引入与标量阻抗子问题相关的块对角预条件子，构建高阶Nyström实现。

Result: 数值实验包括：Fourier-Bessel基准测试、平面波圆圆柱验证、光滑非圆边界测试、条件数与GMRES迭代对比，以及可变阻抗后向散射缩减示例。结果表明该方法作为耦合阻抗系统的前向求解器具有稳定高精度特性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了稳定高精度的前向求解器，但并非新的物理模型，而是在已有公式基础上的高阶数值实现。

Abstract: We study the numerical solution of electromagnetic scattering by an infinitely long impedance cylinder under oblique incidence. After separation of the axial phase factor, the axial electric and magnetic components satisfy a pair of coupled two-dimensional Helmholtz equations. The Leontovich impedance condition couples these components through tangential derivatives, and the associated boundary integral system contains both logarithmic kernels and principal-value tangential derivative terms. Building on existing coupled integral-equation formulations for oblique-incidence cylinder scattering, we construct a high-order Nystrom implementation based on Kress-type logarithmic kernel decomposition, periodic product quadrature, Fourier differentiation for the tangential derivative contribution, and a block diagonal preconditioner associated with the scalar impedance subproblem. Under uniqueness of the continuous scattering problem and a uniform discrete stability assumption, we formulate a high-order convergence framework for the boundary densities and far-field patterns. Numerical experiments include a manufactured Fourier-Bessel benchmark, a plane-wave circular-cylinder validation, a smooth non-circular boundary test, condition-number and GMRES comparisons, and a variable-impedance scattering-width reduction example in a prescribed backward angular sector. The results indicate that the method provides a stable high-accuracy forward solver for the coupled impedance system, rather than a new physical model.

</details>


### [122] [Flowing to Normality and the Fate of the Single Ring Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15791)
*Joshua Feinberg,Roman Riser,Richard Scalettar,Anthony Zee*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类从满足单环定理的非厄米随机矩阵系综流向正规矩阵系综的矩阵模型。通过调节惩罚非正规性的参数，发现当参数达到临界值时单环定理失效，同时奇异值的间距统计从Wigner-Dyson排斥分布连续过渡到Poisson分布，但这一统计转变与单环定理的破坏并不直接相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米随机矩阵中，双端旋转不变系综服从单环定理（支撑集为圆盘或圆环），而旋转不变正规随机矩阵系综的均值特征值密度可支持任意多个同心圆环。作者希望构建一个在这两种行为之间连续过渡的矩阵模型，以理解单环定理的适用范围及其与正规性之间的关系。

Method: 引入一个带参数的矩阵模型，通过调节惩罚非正规性的参数实现从一般非厄米矩阵到正规矩阵的流动。采用解析分析与数值模拟相结合的方法，研究特征值分布、奇异值行为以及参数临界值。

Result: (1) 数值上观察到单环定理随模型流向正规性而失效，确定了发生转变的临界参数值；(2) 奇异值形成受限于正半轴的费米气体，小参数下表现出Wigner-Dyson排斥统计，大参数下（正规矩阵端点）表现为Poisson统计，且该统计转变是连续的；(3) 单环定理的破坏发生在统计转变之前（仍处于Wigner-Dyson统计区域），两者并非直接相关；(4) 引入与气体相关的随机置换系综，并给出利用奇异值密度近似重构大N极限下复特征值平均密度的猜想。

Conclusion: 该模型成功连接了单环定理描述的系综与正规矩阵系综，揭示了单环定理的破坏与奇异值统计转变是两个不同尺度的现象。单环定理在非正规性较弱时即失效，而间距统计的转变需要更强的正规性条件。随机置换系综的引入为从奇异值信息重构特征值分布提供了新思路。

Abstract: Random non-hermitian matrix ensembles with double-sided rotation invariance obey, in the limit of large matrix size, the Single Ring Theorem, which states that the support of the mean eigenvalue distribution in the complex plane is either a disk or an annulus. In contrast, rotational-invariant random normal matrix ensembles can have mean eigenvalue densities supported over any number of concentric annuli in the complex plane. In this paper we introduce and investigate, both analytically and numerically, a non-hermitian matrix model which flows from a generic matrix distribution obeying the Single Ring Theorem to a distribution of normal matrices by tuning a parameter which penalizes non-normality.
  We observe numerically breakdown of the Single Ring Theorem as the model flows towards normality, and determine the critical value of the parameter at which the transition occurs. We also study in detail the behavior of the singular values of these matrices under the flow. These singular values form a Fermi gas confined to the positive half-line. In particular, we find that at small values of the flow parameter, the interparticle spacings in the gas exhibit Wigner-Dyson repulsion, whereas for asymptotically large values of the flow parameter, at the normal matrix endpoint of the flow, the spacing statistics is Poissonian. The flow interpolates continuously between these two types of statistics. However, this change in statistics is not related directly to breaking of the Single Ring Theorem, which occurs very early-on along the flow, in the regime of Wigner-Dyson statistics. Finally, we introduce a certain ensemble of random permutations associated with the gas, and make a conjecture on how to use it in order to reconstruct approximately the average density of complex eigenvalues from that of the singular values in the large-$N$ limit.

</details>


### [123] [N-transform and factorization of the DN-map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15895)
*M. I. Belishev,D. V. Korikov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究3D黎曼流形上的N-变换，证明其是酉算子，并揭示了该变换与DN映射之间的因式分解关系Λ^{-1}=V^*V，为从边界数据反演流形结构提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为从边界DN映射（Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射）反演三维黎曼流形(Ω,g)的内部几何结构，引入一种新的算子分解工具。

Method: 定义势向量场空间P和与梯度∇τ平行的向量场空间L_λ，逐层构造N-变换：Nh|_{Γ^τ}:=(P^τh)|_{Γ^{τ-0}}，其中P^τ是到支撑在Ω^τ内的子空间上的投影。证明N是酉算子，并推导DN映射的因式分解Λ^{-1}=V^*V。

Result: N-变换是一个酉算子；DN映射Λ的逆可以分解为Λ^{-1}=V^*V，其中V与N-变换相关。

Conclusion: N-变换为通过边界DN映射确定(Ω,g)提供了新的理论框架，其因式分解性质可能在反问题中有重要应用。

Abstract: Let $(Ω,g)$ be a smooth compact 3D Riemannian manifold with the smooth boundary $Γ$, $τ(x):={\rm dist\,}(x,Γ)$, $x\inΩ$; $Ω^τ:=\{x\inΩ\,|\,\,{\rm dist\,}(x,Γ)<τ$\}, $Γ^τ:=\{x\inΩ\,|\,\,{\rm dist\,}(x,Γ)=τ$\}, $τ\geqslant 0$. For the sake of technical simplicity, we deal with $Ω$ diffeomorphic to a ball in $\Bbb R^3$.
  Let $\mathscr P:=\{\nabla p\,|\,\,p\in H^1(Ω)\}$ be the space of the potential vector fields, and let $\mathscr L_λ:=\{\varkappa\nablaτ\,|\,\,\varkappa\in L_2(Ω)\}$ be the space of the vector fields parallel to $\nablaτ$. The N-transform is a map from $\mathscr P$ to $\mathscr L_λ$ defined layer-wise (in accordance with $Ω=\cup_{τ\geqslant 0}Γ^τ$) by $$ Nh\,\big|_{Γ^τ}:=(P^τh)\big|_{Γ^{τ-0}}, \qquadτ>0, $$ where $P^τ$ are the projections in $\mathscr P$ onto the subspaces $\mathscr P^τ:=\{h\in\mathscr P\,|\,\,{\rm supp\,}h\subset\overline{Ω^τ}\}$. We show that $N$ is a unitary operator.
  Let $p=p^f(x)$ be a solution to the Dirichlet problem: $Δ_g p=0$ in $Ω\setminusΓ$, $p=f$ on $Γ$. The DN-map $Λ$ is defined by $Λf:=-\langle\nabla p^f,\nablaτ\rangle$ on $Γ$. We show that the N-transform provides a certain factorization $Λ^{-1}=V^*V$ and discuss its possible usefulness for determination of $(Ω,g)$ from $Λ$.

</details>


### [124] [A generalized Stieltjes system with polynomial source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15916)
*D. Masoero,B. Shapiro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个关于N个不同复数x_i的代数系统，其中∑_{j≠i} 1/(x_i-x_j) = Q(x_i)，Q是M+1次首一多项式。证明了该系统解的数量上限为C(N+M, N)，并给出了系数方程的总交重数恰好等于该组合数。当Q的线性项系数很大时，系统会分解为M+1个弱耦合的经典Stieltjes系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Stieltjes系统（M=0情形，对应Hermite多项式的零点）推广到任意首一多项式Q，研究其代数系统的解的数量和结构性质。

Method: 通过代数几何方法，分析系数方程的总交重数，利用Zariski开集论证上界可达性，并通过渐近分析研究Q线性项系数很大时系统的分解行为。

Result: 系统解的数量上界为C(N+M, N)；系数方程的总交重数恰好等于该组合数；该上界在非空Zariski开集上可达；当Q线性项系数很大时，系统分裂为M+1个弱耦合经典Stieltjes系统。

Conclusion: 建立了广义Stieltjes系统解的数量上界与组合数的精确对应关系，证明了上界的普遍可达性，并揭示了系统在大参数极限下的分解结构。

Abstract: Let $Q$ be a monic polynomial of degree $M+1$. We study the algebraic system \[
  \sum_{j\ne i}\frac{1}{x_i-x_j}=Q(x_i),\qquad i=1,\ldots,N, \] for pairwise distinct complex numbers $x_1,\ldots,x_N$, modulo permutations of these numbers. The case $M=0$ is, after a translation, the classical Stieltjes system for the zeros of a Hermite polynomial. We prove that, for arbitrary $Q$, the number of solutions is at most $\binom{N+M}{N}$, and that the coefficient equations for the associated monic Stieltjes polynomial have total intersection multiplicity exactly $\binom{N+M}{N}$. Consequently the bound is attained for all $Q$ in a non-empty Zariski open subset of the affine space of monic polynomials of degree $M+1$. We also describe the solutions when the coefficient of the linear term of $Q$ is large: the system splits into $M+1$ weakly coupled classical Stieltjes systems, one near each zero of $Q$.

</details>


### [125] [$q$-Deformed Topological Recursion, Weight Vectors and Algebraic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16441)
*Fridolin Melong,Raimar Wulkenhaar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了移位拓扑递归的q-变形，将其推广到量子代数框架，通过分析q-变形W-代数表示中的最高权向量，推导出拓扑递归的q-类比，并证明其产生q-变形的量子曲线，统一了量子可积性的多种途径，为q-变形模空间的几何提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Belliard-Bouchard-Kramer-Nelson构建的移位拓扑递归推广到量子代数语境，探索q-变形在量子可积系统和模空间几何中的应用。

Method: 通过研究q-变形W-代数表示中的最高权向量，推导拓扑递归的q-类比，并证明其产生q-变形的量子曲线。

Result: 建立了q-变形的移位拓扑递归框架，得到了q-变形量子曲线，统一了多种量子可积性方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为q-变形模空间几何提供了新见解，推动了量子代数与拓扑递归理论的交叉发展。

Abstract: We investigate a $q$-deformation of the shifted topological recursion, extending the construction of Belliard-Bouchard-Kramer-Nelson to the context of quantum algebras. Through the study of highest weight vectors in $q$-deformed of $\mathcal{W}$-algebra representations, we derive a $q$-analogue of the topological recursion and show it yields $q$-deformed quantum curves. This framework unifies various approaches to quantum integrability and provides new insights into the geometry of $q$-deformed moduli spaces.

</details>


### [126] [Boundary monopole bubbling and Macdonald kernels for non-minuscule 't Hooft lines in $\mathcal N=4$ SYM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16660)
*Ruiliang Li*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维 $\mathcal N=4$ $U(N)$ 超杨-米尔斯理论中非极小磁荷的半BPS边界't Hooft线，计算了气泡修正的缺陷半指标，将其与球面DAHA元 $\mathbf e h_r(Y)\mathbf e$ 等同，通过差分算子展开得到屏蔽磁扇区，并证明了与S-对偶诺伊曼Wilson线半指标的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非极小磁荷边界't Hooft线的 monopole bubbling 贡献，建立其与球面DAHA以及S-对偶Wilson线的精确对应关系。

Method: 对单行荷 $λ=(r,0,\ldots,0)$ 计算气泡修正的缺陷半指标；利用球面DAHA元 $\mathbf e h_r(Y)\mathbf e$ 的差分算子展开得到屏蔽磁扇区；通过Macdonald核证明与S-对偶诺伊曼Wilson线半指标的等价性；应用边界不动点公式实现相同系数。

Result: 得到了 $U(2)$ 所有主导磁荷的恒等式，并给出了秩二和秩三中的显式非极小例子。

Conclusion: 非极小边界't Hooft线可被球面DAHA元精确描述，其S-对偶对应是诺伊曼Wilson线，边界不动点公式有效再现了气泡修正系数。

Abstract: We study half-BPS boundary 't Hooft lines of non-minuscule magnetic charge in four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ $U(N)$ super Yang--Mills theory with the regular Nahm-pole boundary condition. In contrast to minuscule charges, non-minuscule boundary 't Hooft lines receive monopole bubbling contributions. For all one-row charges $λ=(r,0,\ldots,0)$ we compute the bubbling-corrected defect half-index and identify the boundary 't Hooft operator with the spherical DAHA element $\mathbf e h_r(Y)\mathbf e$. Its difference-operator expansion gives the screened magnetic sectors, while the Macdonald kernel proves equality with the S-dual Neumann Wilson-line half-index. As a consequence we obtain the identity for all dominant magnetic charges of $U(2)$. The boundary fixed-point formula realizes the same coefficients and gives explicit non-minuscule examples in ranks two and three.

</details>


### [127] [The Algebra of Units: From Buckingham's Pi-grec Theorem to Latent-Variable Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16737)
*Mauro Valorani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的方法，通过对数变换和奇异值分解（SVD），无需先验物理知识即可从数据中自动发现系统的无量纲数（如流量系数、扬程系数和马赫数），在合成压缩机数据集上验证了方法的有效性，并揭示了量纲分析与现代数据驱动学习之间的代数联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上识别无量纲数需要专家知识和物理洞察，本文希望从数据中自动发现这些无量纲数，无需了解控制物理原理。

Method: 对测量数据进行对数变换，利用奇异值分解（SVD）识别低维流形；通过整数指数组合搜索恢复候选无量纲量；使用重复变量过滤保留由机器特征尺度构建的无量纲组。

Result: 在包含16,000个测量值的合成压缩机数据集上，从原始有量纲变量出发，无需任何物理输入，恢复出正确的无量纲组达到数值精度，重现压缩机性能图的误差低于0.01%。

Conclusion: 经典量纲分析与现代数据驱动学习共享相同的代数结构，为构建兼具可解释性、可扩展性和数据效率的物理模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Engineers often measure many quantities-speed, pressure, temperature, length-expressed in different physical units. The Buckingham Pi-grec theorem states that these variables can always be combined into a smaller set of dimensionless numbers whose values fully determine the system's behaviour.
  Identifying the appropriate dimensionless groups has traditionally required expert knowledge and physical insight. This paper shows that they can instead be discovered automatically from data, without prior knowledge of the governing physics.
  The key observation is that, after logarithmic transformation, measurements collected under different scalings of the same system lie on a low-dimensional manifold whose geometry is determined by the underlying dimensionless groups. Singular value decomposition (SVD) identifies this manifold directly from data. A subsequent search over integer-exponent combinations recovers candidate dimensionless quantities, while a repeating-variable filter retains only those constructed from the machine's characteristic scales. This procedure recovers familiar engineering groups, including the flow coefficient, head coefficient, and Mach number, while excluding equivalent but less interpretable alternatives.
  The method is demonstrated on a synthetic compressor dataset containing 16,000 measurements. Starting from raw dimensional variables and no physics input, it recovers the correct dimensionless groups to numerical precision and reproduces the compressor performance map with an error below 0.01%.
  More broadly, the work reveals a close connection between classical dimensional analysis and modern data-driven learning. Both rely on the same underlying algebraic structure, suggesting new approaches for building physical models that are simultaneously interpretable, scalable, and data-efficient.

</details>


### [128] [Self-Adjointness of the Standard Model of Non-Relativistic QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16947)
*Valentin Kußmaul*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过比较图范数和Nelson交换子定理，为非相对论带电粒子系统与量子化辐射场软模最小耦合的哈密顿量在自由哈密顿量定义域上的自伴性提供了一个简化的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然该结果并非首次提出，但作者旨在简化已有的证明方法，使得证明过程更加简短和高效。

Method: 采用比较图范数（graph norms）的技巧，结合Nelson交换子定理（Nelson's commutator theorem）来证明自伴性。

Result: 成功证明系统哈密顿量在自由哈密顿量定义域上具有自伴性（self-adjointness）。

Conclusion: 所提出的证明比先前的方法更为简洁，为量子辐射场中带电粒子系统的数学基础提供了更高效的论证路径。

Abstract: For systems of non-relativistic charged particles minimally coupled to the soft modes of the quantized radiation field, we prove self-adjointness on the domain of the free Hamiltonian. This result is not new, but the proof we give shortens previous arguments. It is based on a comparison of graph norms and Nelson's commutator theorem.

</details>
